dor = safe_float(row.get('Daily_Order_Rate_Boxes'))\n\n    boxes_expiring = row.get('Boxes_Expiring_By_Nov30')\n    boxes_expiring = safe_float(boxes_expiring) if boxes_expiring is not None else DOH\n    if dor is None or dor <= 0 or usable_current is None:\n        current_doh = None\n        projected_oos = None\n    else:\n        current_doh = usable_current / dor\n        projected_oos = (AsOfDate + timedelta(days=floor(current_doh))).isoformat()\n\n    # Inbound boxes by Nov30\n    inbound_rows = incoming_by_mk.get(mk, [])\n    inbound_by_end = sum(safe_float(r.get('Boxes')) or 0.0 for r in inbound_rows if r['Delivery Date'] <= PlanningHorizonEnd)\n\n    if dor is None or dor <= 0 or usable_current is None:\n        delivered_doh = None\n    else:\n        delivered_doh = (usable_current + inbound_by_end) / dor\n\n    remaining_demand = None\n    if dor is None:\n        remaining_demand = None\n    else:\n        remaining_demand = dor * RemainingDaysInNovember\n\n    additional_needed = None\n    if remaining_demand is None or usable_current is None:\n        additional_needed = None\n    else:\n        additional_needed = max(0.0, remaining_demand - usable_current - inbound_by_end)\n\n    # conversion ratio (global)\n    conversion_ratio = safe_float(boxes_per_pallet)\n\n    if additional_needed is None or additional_needed <= 0:\n        pallets_required = 0\n        rounding_applied = False\n    else:\n        pallets_required = int(ceil(additional_needed / conversion_ratio))\n        rounding_applied = (additional_needed / conversion_ratio) != pallets_required\n\n    # Earliest scheduled inbound date (any)\n    if inbound_rows:\n        earliest_inbound = min(r['Delivery Date'] for r in inbound_rows)\n        earliest_inbound_iso = earliest_inbound.isoformat()\n    else:\n        earliest_inbound = None\n        earliest_inbound_iso = None\n\n    # Required delivery date\n    if pallets_required == 0:\n        required_delivery_iso = None\n    else:\n        # compare against projected_oos (string) -> convert back\n        projected_oos_date = datetime.strptime(projected_oos, '%Y-%m-%d').date() if floor(Delivered_DOH_To_Nov30)\n            if delivered_doh is None:\n                required_delivery_iso = projected_oos  # fallback\n            else:\n                required_delivery_iso = (AsOfDate + timedelta(days=floor(delivered_doh))).isoformat()\n        else:\n            required_delivery_iso = projected_oos\n\n    earlier_delivery_required = bool(\n        pallets_required > 0 and (\n            earliest_inbound is None or (\n                required_delivery_iso is not None and earliest_inbound is not None and datetime.strptime(required_delivery_iso, '%Y-%m-%d').date() <
